# Segment 119 — Test Case Cards

One card per test case (QE feedback Def_SepRun07/Def_SepRun10). Derived from the canonical JSON on every run; JSON stays authoritative. Fields flagged UNTRUSTED_VALID_VALUES, AMBIGUOUS_LENGTH or DELIBERATE_CONSTRAINT_VIOLATION are marked ⚠.

## TC-003462 — Negative — Financial Transaction Request — Segment Type

**Purpose:** In Totals with Proprietary Data Load Data Segment (Data Segment No. 119), field 1 (Segment Type) is required and must be present. (source [p.260](../../../../../docs/specs/BUYPASS_Platform_ATL105_Message_Format_Specifications_2026-3.pdf#page=260))
